## Embedded Files
c6185552724528eda466a99b9b611e0f62e5cefd: $$p(x;\theta)=\frac{q(x;\theta)}{Z(\theta)}$$

d67df0edf3a7ea101f6fdc295bf2137a27d58f72: $$\nabla_{x} \log(p(x))$$

e81723c2e7ee6a9a65085dad28f344ef7b664ee8: $$\nabla_{x} \log\left(\frac{q(x)}{Z}\right)=\nabla_{x} \log(q(x)) - \nabla_x \log(Z) = \nabla_{x} \log(q(x))$$

e3c2959977eab46028ca4265951f9a8d3958b680: $$\frac{1}{2} \mathbb{E}_{p_{\text{data}}} \left[ \| \mathbf{s}_\theta(\mathbf{x}) - \nabla_{\mathbf{x}} \log p_{\text{data}}(\mathbf{x}) \|_2^2 \right]$$

dcb2a0af259c1a90b3e1267d22dcb900a0def400: $$\mathbb{E}_{p_{\text{data}}(\mathbf{x})} \left[ \mathrm{tr}(\nabla_{\mathbf{x}} \mathbf{s}_{\theta}(\mathbf{x})) + \frac{1}{2} \| \mathbf{s}_{\theta}(\mathbf{x}) \|_2^2 \right]$$

24762274d708fe2e9e414120ea6909a0e2b71e6d: $$\mathbb{E}_{p_{\mathbf{v}}} \mathbb{E}_{p_{\text{data}}} \left[ \mathbf{v}^\top \nabla_{\mathbf{x}} \mathbf{s}_{\theta}(\mathbf{x}) \mathbf{v} + \frac{1}{2} \| \mathbf{s}_{\theta}(\mathbf{x}) \|_2^2 \right]$$

e68fe29f9fc3e14d3205fe9d59ed3e41b599dab8: $$q_\sigma(\tilde x|x)$$

aa0c256f63f0c6859a7d37dab2ec0998b7966275: $$q_\sigma(\tilde x)=\int q_\sigma(\tilde x|x)p_{\text{data}}(x) \, dx$$

fb0689715a04541a977e7ec48a50e8727d1fa707: $$\frac{1}{2} \mathbb{E}_{q_{\sigma}(\tilde{\mathbf{x}}|\mathbf{x}) p_{\text{data}}(\mathbf{x})} \left[ \| \mathbf{s}_{\theta}(\tilde{\mathbf{x}}) - \nabla_{\tilde{\mathbf{x}}} \log q_{\sigma}(\tilde{\mathbf{x}} | \mathbf{x}) \|_2^2 \right]$$

afd19066e333c68434123f228ab712c5fa96a750: $$s_\theta(x,\sigma)$$

4f3c44ddacd6eb4cfda25139f6fc39dadf9095d9: $$\{\sigma_i\}^L_{i=1}$$

fff875a0b894eec66636ef097b84ff1fce3d82a7: $$\frac{\sigma_{i-1}}{\sigma_i}=K<1$$

010faea71da8c39c5640629dec279fed7fb5067e: $$\mathbf{s}_{\theta^*}(\mathbf{x}) = \nabla_{\mathbf{x}} \log q_{\sigma}(\mathbf{x})$$

9bcd47695b1d35734e3e26f77948667fcce62a5c: $$q_{\sigma}(\tilde{\mathbf{x}} | \mathbf{x}) = \mathcal{N}(\tilde{\mathbf{x}} | \mathbf{x}, \sigma^2 I)$$

565316e63982a27841f366498f3bb6f8a176ee02: $$q_\sigma(\tilde x)=\int p_{\text{data}}(x) \mathcal{N}(\tilde{x}|x,\sigma^2 I) \, dx$$

f300d7820ffb4a14de75ea2b53da2d6b3cc1c32c: $$\nabla_x\log q_\sigma (\tilde{x}|x)=-\frac{\tilde{x}-x}{\sigma^2}$$

dee50b26dfa1b40c62b48ce8eb2db8d9d69b41be: $$\ell(\boldsymbol{\theta}; \sigma) =\frac{1}{2} \mathbb{E}_{p_{\text{data}}(\mathbf{x})} \mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\mathbf{x}} \sim \mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x}, \sigma^2 I)} \left[ \left\| \mathbf{s}_{\theta}(\tilde{\mathbf{x}}, \sigma) + \frac{\tilde{\mathbf{x}} - \mathbf{x}}{\sigma^2} \right\|_2^2 \right]$$

a19d09f16fa81d5e975efd8f55fdb13fbbbde85a: $$\mathcal{L}(\boldsymbol{\theta}; \{\sigma_i\}_{i=1}^L) = \frac{1}{L} \sum_{i=1}^L \lambda(\sigma_i) \ell(\boldsymbol{\theta}; \sigma_i)$$

972959cb9fb28b9b064212734c2bf132d4bb47e2: $$\begin{array}{l}
\textbf{Require: } \{\sigma_i\}_{i=1}^L, \epsilon, T. \\
\text{Initialize } \tilde{\mathbf{x}}_0 \\
\textbf{for } i \leftarrow 1 \textbf{ to } L \textbf{ do} \\
\quad \alpha_i \leftarrow \epsilon \cdot \sigma_i^2 / \sigma_L^2 \\
\quad \textbf{for } t \leftarrow 1 \textbf{ to } T \textbf{ do} \\
\quad \quad \text{Draw } \mathbf{z}_t \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I) \\
\quad \quad \tilde{\mathbf{x}}_t \leftarrow \tilde{\mathbf{x}}_{t-1} + \frac{\alpha_i}{2} \mathbf{s}_{\theta}(\tilde{\mathbf{x}}_{t-1}, \sigma_i) + \sqrt{\alpha_i} \mathbf{z}_t \\
\quad \textbf{end for} \\
\quad \tilde{\mathbf{x}}_0 \leftarrow \tilde{\mathbf{x}}_T \\
\textbf{end for} \\
\textbf{return } \tilde{\mathbf{x}}_T
\end{array}$$
